  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Forretress ex</strong><br><em>From Paldea Evolved</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Ability: Exploding Energy<br>Once during your turn, you may search your deck for up to 5 Basic [G] Energy cards and attach them to your Pokémon in any way you like. </div>




















  
  



</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="8c7e38ca1a4be145db9c" id="block-8c7e38ca1a4be145db9c"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Artazon</strong><br><em>From Paldea Evolved</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Once during each player's turn, that player may search their deck for a Basic Pokémon that doesn’t have a Rule Box and put it onto their Bench. Then, that player shuffles their deck. <em>(Pokémon ex, Pokémon V, etc. have Rule Boxes.)</em></p>
</div>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Atticus</strong><br><em>From Gym Promos</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">You can use this card only if your opponent’s Active Pokémon is Poisoned.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Shuffle your hand into your deck, then draw 7 cards.</p>

  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Electric Generator</strong><br><em>From Scarlet &amp; Violet</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Look at the top 5 cards of your deck and attach up to 2 Basic [L] Energy cards you find there to your Benched [L] Pokémon in any way you like.
